===== BEGIN FILE: src/server/workspace.ts =====
import sanitize from 'sanitize-html';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { AppError, denied } from './errors';
import { Odoo, relationId, selectFields, type RecordData, type Fields } from './odoo';
import { config } from './config';
import { pool } from './db';

export const cleanHtml = (value: unknown) =>
  sanitize(typeof value === 'string' ? value : '', {
    allowedTags: [
      'p',
      'br',
      'b',
      'strong',
      'em',
      'i',
      'u',
      'ul',
      'ol',
      'li',
      'blockquote',
      'pre',
      'code',
      'h2',
      'h3',
      'a',
    ],
    allowedAttributes: { a: ['href', 'title'] },
    allowedSchemes: ['https', 'http', 'mailto'],
    transformTags: {
      a: sanitize.simpleTransform('a', { rel: 'noopener noreferrer', target: '_blank' }),
    },
  });
export const plainText = (value: unknown) =>
  sanitize(typeof value === 'string' ? value : '', { allowedTags: [], allowedAttributes: {} });
export const normalize = (value: string) =>
  plainText(value)
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .replace(/đ/g, 'd')
    .replace(/Đ/g, 'D')
    .toLocaleLowerCase('vi')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
const taskFields = [
  'id',
  'name',
  'project_id',
  'parent_id',
  'stage_id',
  'state',
  'user_ids',
  'date_deadline',
  'priority',
  'description',
  'write_date',
  'workspace_revision',
  'child_ids',
];

export async function taskDomain(
  odoo: Odoo,
  params: URLSearchParams,
  uid: number,
  metadata: Fields,
) {
  const domain: unknown[] = [];
  if (params.get('project'))
    domain.push([
      'project_id',
      '=',
      z.coerce.number().int().positive().parse(params.get('project')),
    ]);
  if (params.get('stage'))
    domain.push(['stage_id', '=', z.coerce.number().int().positive().parse(params.get('stage'))]);
  if (params.get('mine') === 'true') domain.push(['user_ids', 'in', [uid]]);
  if (params.get('assignee'))
    domain.push([
      'user_ids',
      'in',
      [z.coerce.number().int().positive().parse(params.get('assignee'))],
    ]);
  if (params.get('state')) {
    const state = z.string().max(80).parse(params.get('state'));
    if (!metadata.state?.selection?.some(([key]) => key === state))
      throw new AppError(400, 'INVALID_STATE', 'Trạng thái không có trong cấu hình Odoo.');
    domain.push(['state', '=', state]);
  }
  if (params.get('open') === 'true') {
    const closed =
      metadata.state?.selection
        ?.map(([v]) => v)
        .filter((v) => ['1_done', '1_canceled'].includes(v)) || [];
    if (closed.length) domain.push(['state', 'not in', closed]);
  }
  if (params.get('overdue') === 'true') {
    if (!metadata.date_deadline)
      throw new AppError(400, 'UNSUPPORTED_FILTER', 'Odoo không có trường hạn nhiệm vụ.');
    const user = await odoo.read('res.users', uid, ['tz']);
    let today: string;
    try {
      today = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
        timeZone: typeof user.tz === 'string' ? user.tz : 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh',
      }).format(new Date());
    } catch {
      today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    }
    domain.push(['date_deadline', '<', today]);
    const closed =
      metadata.state?.selection
        ?.map(([v]) => v)
        .filter((v) => ['1_done', '1_canceled'].includes(v)) || [];
    if (closed.length) domain.push(['state', 'not in', closed]);
  }
  return domain;
}
export async function decorateTasks(odoo: Odoo, rows: RecordData[], metadata: Fields) {
  const ids = [
    ...new Set(rows.flatMap((r) => (Array.isArray(r.user_ids) ? (r.user_ids as number[]) : []))),
  ];
  let users: RecordData[] = [];
  if (ids.length)
    try {
      users = await odoo.search('res.users', [['id', 'in', ids]], ['name'], 200);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof AppError && e.status === 403)) throw e;
    }
  return rows.map((row) => ({
    ...row,
    stateLabel: metadata.state?.selection?.find(([key]) => key === row.state)?.[1] || '',
    assignees: ((row.user_ids as number[]) || []).map((id) => ({
      id,
      name: users.find((u) => u.id === id)?.name || `Nhân viên #${id}`,
    })),
  }));
}
export async function taskOptions(odoo: Odoo) {
  const fields = await odoo.fields('project.task');
  const [projects, stages, users] = await Promise.all([
    odoo.search('project.project', [], ['name'], 100, 0, 'name'),
    odoo.search('project.task.type', [], ['name'], 100, 0, 'sequence,id'),
    odoo.search('res.users', [['share', '=', false]], ['name'], 200, 0, 'name').catch((e) => {
      if (e instanceof AppError && e.status === 403) return [];
      throw e;
    }),
  ]);
  return { projects, stages, users, states: fields.state?.selection || [] };
}
export async function taskList(odoo: Odoo, params: URLSearchParams, uid: number) {
  const metadata = await odoo.fields('project.task');
  const fields = selectFields(
    metadata,
    taskFields.filter((f) => f !== 'description' && f !== 'child_ids'),
  );
  const domain = await taskDomain(odoo, params, uid, metadata);
  const cursor = z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(0)
    .max(1000000)
    .parse(params.get('cursor') || 0);
  const query = (params.get('q') || '').trim();
  if (query.length > 160) throw new AppError(400, 'QUERY_TOO_LONG', 'Từ khóa tối đa 160 ký tự.');
  if (!query) {
    const order =
      params.get('sort') === 'updated'
        ? 'write_date desc,id desc'
        : params.get('sort') === 'deadline'
          ? 'date_deadline asc,id desc'
          : 'id desc';
    const rows = await odoo.search('project.task', domain, fields, 41, cursor, order);
    return {
      items: await decorateTasks(odoo, rows.slice(0, 40), metadata),
      nextCursor: rows.length > 40 ? cursor + 40 : null,
      mode: 'orm',
      scanLimited: false,
    };
  }
  // Per-user live ORM scan: accent-independent matching without a shared permission cache.
  // A bounded scan returns an explicit continuation, never a false complete result set.
  const descriptions = params.get('description') === 'true';
  const q = normalize(query);
  const found: RecordData[] = [];
  let offset = cursor;
  let exhausted = false;
  let scanned = 0;
  while (scanned < 1000 && found.length < 40) {
    const rows = await odoo.search(
      'project.task',
      domain,
      [...fields, ...(descriptions && metadata.description ? ['description'] : [])],
      100,
      offset,
      'id asc',
    );
    if (!rows.length) {
      exhausted = true;
      break;
    }
    for (const task of rows) {
      offset++;
      scanned++;
      const project = Array.isArray(task.project_id) ? String(task.project_id[1]) : '';
      if (
        [
          task.name || '',
          project,
          String(task.id),
          ...(descriptions ? [String(task.description || '')] : []),
        ].some((s) => normalize(s).includes(q))
      ) {
        const { description: _removed, ...safe } = task;
        void _removed;
        found.push(safe as RecordData);
      }
      if (found.length === 40) break;
    }
    if (rows.length < 100 && found.length < 40) {
      exhausted = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  return {
    items: await decorateTasks(odoo, found, metadata),
    nextCursor: exhausted ? null : offset,
    mode: 'live-accent-scan',
    scanLimited: !exhausted,
    scanned,
  };
}

export async function taskDetail(odoo: Odoo, id: number, identity: string) {
  const metadata = await odoo.fields('project.task');
  const task = await odoo.read('project.task', id, selectFields(metadata, taskFields));
  task.description = cleanHtml(task.description);
  const ancestors: RecordData[] = [];
  const visited = new Set([id]);
  let parent = relationId(task.parent_id);
  let parentUnavailable = false;
  while (parent && ancestors.length < 20 && !visited.has(parent)) {
    visited.add(parent);
    try {
      const row = await odoo.read('project.task', parent, ['name', 'parent_id', 'project_id']);
      ancestors.unshift(row);
      parent = relationId(row.parent_id);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof AppError && [403, 404].includes(e.status)) {
        parentUnavailable = true;
        break;
      }
      throw e;
    }
  }
  const children = await odoo.search(
    'project.task',
    [['parent_id', '=', id]],
    selectFields(metadata, ['name', 'project_id', 'stage_id', 'state', 'user_ids']),
    100,
  );
  const siblings = relationId(task.parent_id)
    ? await odoo.search(
        'project.task',
        [
          ['parent_id', '=', relationId(task.parent_id)],
          ['id', '!=', id],
        ],
        ['name', 'project_id'],
        40,
      )
    : [];
  const projects = relationId(task.project_id);
  const stageMeta = await odoo.fields('project.task.type');
  const stages = await odoo.search(
    'project.task.type',
    stageMeta.project_ids && projects
      ? ['|', ['project_ids', 'in', [projects]], ['project_ids', '=', false]]
      : [],
    selectFields(stageMeta, ['name', 'sequence']),
    100,
    0,
    'sequence,id',
  );
  let users: RecordData[] = [];
  try {
    users = await odoo.search('res.users', [['share', '=', false]], ['name'], 200, 0, 'name');
  } catch (e) {
    if (!(e instanceof AppError && e.status === 403)) throw e;
  }
  await pool().query(
    `INSERT INTO workspace_preferences(identity,task_id) VALUES($1,$2) ON CONFLICT(identity,task_id) DO UPDATE SET visited_at=now()`,
    [identity, id],
  );
  return {
    task,
    ancestors,
    parentUnavailable,
    children,
    siblings,
    stages,
    users,
    fields: metadata,
    canAtomicEdit: !!metadata.workspace_revision && config().write,
    writesEnabled: config().write,
    odooLink: `${config().publicUrl}/web?db=${encodeURIComponent(config().db)}#id=${id}&model=project.task&view_type=form`,
  };
}

export const editTaskSchema = z
  .object({
    expectedRevision: z.string().min(1).max(100),
    values: z
      .object({
        name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(500).optional(),
        description: z.string().max(50000).optional(),
        stage_id: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
        state: z.string().max(80).optional(),
        user_ids: z.array(z.number().int().positive()).max(50).optional(),
        date_deadline: z.union([z.iso.date(), z.literal(false)]).optional(),
        priority: z.string().max(20).optional(),
      })
      .strict(),
  })
  .strict();
export async function editTask(odoo: Odoo, id: number, input: z.infer<typeof editTaskSchema>) {
  const fields = await odoo.fields('project.task');
  if (!fields.workspace_revision)
    throw new AppError(
      409,
      'BRIDGE_REQUIRED',
      'Cần addon Smart Workspace Bridge để lưu an toàn khi nhiều người cùng sửa. Bạn có thể mở nhiệm vụ trong Odoo để chỉnh sửa.',
    );
  const values: Record<string, unknown> = { ...input.values };
  if (!Object.keys(values).length) throw new AppError(400, 'EMPTY_UPDATE', 'Chưa có thay đổi.');
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(values)) {
    if (!fields[key] || fields[key].readonly)
      throw new AppError(400, 'UNSUPPORTED_FIELD', 'Trường này không cho phép chỉnh sửa.');
    if (fields[key].selection && !fields[key].selection!.some(([k]) => k === value))
      throw new AppError(400, 'INVALID_SELECTION', 'Giá trị không có trong cấu hình Odoo.');
  }
  if (input.values.description !== undefined)
    values.description = cleanHtml(input.values.description);
  if (input.values.user_ids) values.user_ids = [[6, 0, input.values.user_ids]];
  await odoo.call('project.task', 'workspace_write', [[id], input.expectedRevision, values]);
  return { saved: true };
}
export async function chatter(odoo: Odoo, id: number, focusId?: number) {
  await odoo.requireRecord('project.task', id);
  const rows = await odoo.search(
    'mail.message',
    [
      ['model', '=', 'project.task'],
      ['res_id', '=', id],
    ],
    ['body', 'author_id', 'date', 'message_type', 'subtype_id'],
    100,
    0,
    'id desc',
  );
  if (focusId && !rows.some((row) => row.id === focusId)) {
    const row = await odoo.read('mail.message', focusId, [
      'body',
      'author_id',
      'date',
      'message_type',
      'subtype_id',
      'model',
      'res_id',
    ]);
    if (row.model !== 'project.task' || row.res_id !== id) throw denied();
    rows.unshift(row);
  }
  return rows.map((row) => ({ ...row, body: cleanHtml(row.body) }));
}
export async function attachments(odoo: Odoo, id: number) {
  await odoo.requireRecord('project.task', id);
  return odoo.search(
    'ir.attachment',
    [
      ['res_model', '=', 'project.task'],
      ['res_id', '=', id],
      ['type', '=', 'binary'],
    ],
    ['name', 'mimetype', 'file_size', 'create_date'],
    100,
  );
}
export async function attachment(odoo: Odoo, taskId: number, id: number) {
  await odoo.requireRecord('project.task', taskId);
  const row = await odoo.read('ir.attachment', id, [
    'name',
    'res_model',
    'res_id',
    'datas',
    'mimetype',
    'type',
  ]);
  if (row.res_model !== 'project.task' || row.res_id !== taskId || row.type !== 'binary')
    throw denied();
  return row;
}
export async function notifications(odoo: Odoo, uid: number) {
  const user = await odoo.read('res.users', uid, ['partner_id']);
  try {
    const rows = await odoo.search(
      'mail.notification',
      [['res_partner_id', '=', relationId(user.partner_id)]],
      ['mail_message_id', 'is_read', 'notification_type'],
      80,
    );
    const items = [];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const messageId = relationId(row.mail_message_id);
      if (!messageId) continue;
      try {
        const message = await odoo.read('mail.message', messageId, [
          'body',
          'model',
          'res_id',
          'author_id',
          'date',
          'subtype_id',
        ]);
        if (message.model === 'discuss.channel') {
          const channel = await odoo.read('discuss.channel', Number(message.res_id), [
            'name',
            'is_member',
          ]);
          if (!channel.is_member) continue;
          items.push({
            ...row,
            message: { ...message, body: plainText(message.body) },
            channel,
            targetName: channel.name,
            kind: 'channel',
            href: `/discuss/${channel.id}?message=${message.id}`,
          });
          continue;
        }
        if (message.model !== 'project.task') continue;
        const task = await odoo.read('project.task', Number(message.res_id), [
          'name',
          'project_id',
        ]);
        items.push({
          ...row,
          message: { ...message, body: plainText(message.body) },
          task,
          targetName: task.name,
          kind: 'task',
          href: `/tasks/${task.id}?tab=discussion&message=${message.id}`,
        });
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof AppError && [403, 404].includes(e.status)) continue;
        throw e;
      }
    }
    return { available: true, items };
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof AppError && e.status === 403)
      return {
        available: false,
        items: [],
        reason:
          'Odoo chưa cho phép tài khoản này đọc mail.notification qua External API. Hãy dùng hộp thư Odoo gốc.',
      };
    throw e;
  }
}

import { test, expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { authenticate, Odoo } from '../../src/server/odoo';
async function signIn(page: Page, user: 'A' | 'B') {
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page
    .getByLabel('Email hoặc tên đăng nhập')
    .fill(`workspace.${user.toLowerCase()}@example.test`);
  await page
    .getByLabel('Mật khẩu hoặc API key cá nhân')
    .fill(process.env[`LOCAL_TEST_PASSWORD_${user}`]!);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng nhập', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('heading', { name: user === 'A' ? /Chào Anh/ : /Chào Bảo/ }),
  ).toBeVisible();
}
test('two employees: private ACL, notification, polling sync, stale edit conflict and independent sessions', async ({
  browser,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(100000);
  if (
    !['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(
      new URL(process.env.ODOO_URL || 'https://invalid').hostname,
    ) ||
    process.env.ODOO_DB !== 'workspace_test'
  )
    throw new Error('Requires isolated local workspace_test.');
  const admin = new Odoo({
    uid: await authenticate('admin', process.env.LOCAL_ODOO_ADMIN_PASSWORD!),
    password: process.env.LOCAL_ODOO_ADMIN_PASSWORD!,
  });
  const ids = JSON.parse(
    await admin.call<string>('ir.config_parameter', 'get_param', ['workspace.fixture']),
  );
  const contexts = await Promise.all([
    browser.newContext({
      baseURL: process.env.APP_ORIGIN || 'http://localhost:3000',
      viewport: { width: 1366, height: 768 },
    }),
    browser.newContext({
      baseURL: process.env.APP_ORIGIN || 'http://localhost:3000',
      viewport: { width: 1366, height: 768 },
    }),
  ]);
  const [a, b] = await Promise.all(contexts.map((c) => c.newPage()));
  const errors: string[] = [];
  a.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(e.message));
  b.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(e.message));
  const original = await admin.read('project.task', ids.child, ['description']);
  try {
    await signIn(a, 'A');
    await signIn(b, 'B');
    const [meA, meB] = await Promise.all([a.request.get('/api/me'), b.request.get('/api/me')]);
    expect((await meA.json()).user.id).not.toBe((await meB.json()).user.id);
    await a.goto(`/tasks/${ids.secret}`);
    await expect(a.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Kiểm tra hồ sơ mật', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await b.goto('/search?q=ho%20so%20mat');
    await expect(b.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Chưa có nhiệm vụ phù hợp' })).toBeVisible();
    await b.goto(`/tasks/${ids.secret}`);
    await expect(b.locator('.error-box[role=alert]')).toContainText('Odoo từ chối truy cập');
    await a.goto(`/tasks/${ids.child}?tab=discussion`);
    await b.goto(`/tasks/${ids.child}?tab=discussion`);
    const marker = `Collaboration ${Date.now()}`;
    await b.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Nội dung trao đổi' }).fill(marker);
    await b.getByRole('button', { name: 'Gửi', exact: true }).click();
    await expect(b.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Nội dung trao đổi' })).toHaveValue('');
    // Wait for the actual 30-second query interval on employee A's page.
    await expect(a.locator('.messages')).toContainText(marker, { timeout: 35000 });
    const aUser = await admin.read('res.users', ids.users[0], ['partner_id']);
    const bOdoo = new Odoo({ uid: ids.users[1], password: process.env.LOCAL_TEST_PASSWORD_B! });
    const message = await bOdoo.call<number>('project.task', 'message_post', [[ids.child]], {
      body: `Targeted ${marker}`,
      message_type: 'comment',
      subtype_xmlid: 'mail.mt_comment',
      partner_ids: [(aUser.partner_id as number[])[0]],
    });
    await a.goto('/notifications');
    const target = a.locator(`a[href="/tasks/${ids.child}?tab=discussion&message=${message}"]`);
    await expect(target).toBeVisible();
    await target.click();
    await expect(a.locator(`#message-${message}`)).toHaveClass(/highlighted/);
    const reply = `Reply ${marker}`;
    await a.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Nội dung trao đổi' }).fill(reply);
    await a.getByRole('button', { name: 'Gửi', exact: true }).click();
    await expect(b.locator('.messages')).toContainText(reply, { timeout: 35000 });
    await a.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
    await b.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
    await a.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' }).fill(`Edit A ${marker}`);
    await b.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' }).fill(`Edit B ${marker}`);
    await a.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu vào Odoo', exact: true }).click();
    await expect(a.getByText(`Edit A ${marker}`, { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await b.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu vào Odoo', exact: true }).click();
    await expect(b.getByRole('status')).toContainText('đã được người khác sửa');
    await expect(b.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' })).toContainText(
      `Edit B ${marker}`,
    );
    expect(
      String((await admin.read('project.task', ids.child, ['description'])).description),
    ).toContain(`Edit A ${marker}`);
    const tab = await contexts[0].newPage();
    await tab.goto(`/tasks/${ids.child}`);
    await expect(
      tab.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Kiểm tra hồ sơ thiết kế', exact: true }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await expect(tab.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Đường dẫn nhiệm vụ' })).toContainText(
      'Rà soát hồ sơ',
    );
    await tab.reload();
    await expect(tab.getByText(`Edit A ${marker}`, { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    expect(errors).toEqual([]);
  } finally {
    await admin.call('project.task', 'write', [[ids.child], { description: original.description }]);
    await Promise.all(contexts.map((c) => c.close()));
  }
});

test('login, unaccented search, cross-project navigation, refresh and back', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 1000 });
  const errors: string[] = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(e.message));
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('Email hoặc tên đăng nhập').fill('workspace.a@example.test');
  await page.getByLabel('Mật khẩu hoặc API key cá nhân').fill(process.env.LOCAL_TEST_PASSWORD_A!);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng nhập', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: /Chào Anh/ })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: /Kiểm tra hồ sơ thiết kế/ }).first()).toBeVisible();
  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/desktop-workspace.png', fullPage: true });
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Tìm nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
  await page
    .getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Từ khóa tìm nhiệm vụ' })
    .fill('kiem tra ho so thiet ke');
  await page
    .getByRole('option', { name: /Kiểm tra hồ sơ thiết kế/ })
    .first()
    .click();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Kiểm tra hồ sơ thiết kế', exact: true }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  const taskUrl = page.url();
  await expect(page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Đường dẫn nhiệm vụ' })).toContainText(
    'Nhà máy B',
  );
  await expect(page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Đường dẫn nhiệm vụ' })).toContainText(
    'Rà soát hồ sơ',
  );
  await page.reload();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Kiểm tra hồ sơ thiết kế', exact: true }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await page
    .getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Đường dẫn nhiệm vụ' })
    .getByRole('link', { name: 'Rà soát hồ sơ' })
    .click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Rà soát hồ sơ', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await page.goBack();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(taskUrl);
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Kiểm tra hồ sơ thiết kế', exact: true }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Tài liệu', exact: true }).last().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Tài liệu nhiệm vụ' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng xuất' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login/);
  expect(errors).toEqual([]);
});

test('task edits, chatter draft and file upload reach real Odoo', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('Email hoặc tên đăng nhập').fill('workspace.a@example.test');
  await page.getByLabel('Mật khẩu hoặc API key cá nhân').fill(process.env.LOCAL_TEST_PASSWORD_A!);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng nhập', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: /Chào Anh/ })).toBeVisible();
  await page
    .getByRole('link', { name: /Kiểm tra hồ sơ thiết kế/ })
    .first()
    .click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
  const original = await page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' }).innerHTML();
  const marker = `Browser acceptance ${Date.now()}`;
  await page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' }).fill(marker);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu vào Odoo', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText(marker, { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Nội dung trao đổi' }).fill(marker);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Gửi', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Nội dung trao đổi' })).toHaveValue('');
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Tài liệu', exact: true }).last().click();
  await page.getByLabel('Tải tệp đính kèm').setInputFiles({
    name: 'browser-acceptance.txt',
    mimeType: 'text/plain',
    buffer: Buffer.from(marker),
  });
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: /browser-acceptance.txt/ }).first()).toBeVisible();
  await page.reload();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' }).evaluate((el, html) => {
    el.innerHTML = html;
    el.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('input', { bubbles: true, inputType: 'insertText' }));
  }, original);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu vào Odoo', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
});

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/fixtures/diagnostics.ts =====
import { test as base, expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
const diagnostics = new WeakMap<
  Page,
  {
    js: string[];
    console: string[];
    network: { path: string; status?: number; failure?: string }[];
  }
>();
export const test = base.extend({
  page: async ({ page }, use, testInfo) => {
    const log = {
      js: [] as string[],
      console: [] as string[],
      network: [] as { path: string; status?: number; failure?: string }[],
    };
    diagnostics.set(page, log);
    page.on('pageerror', (e) => log.js.push(e.message));
    page.on('console', (message) => {
      if (message.type() === 'error') log.console.push(message.text());
    });
    page.on('response', (response) => {
      if (response.status() >= 400)
        log.network.push({ path: new URL(response.url()).pathname, status: response.status() });
    });
    page.on('requestfailed', (request) =>
      log.network.push({
        path: new URL(request.url()).pathname,
        failure: request.failure()?.errorText,
      }),
    );
    await use(page);
    await testInfo.attach('browser-diagnostics', {
      body: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(log, null, 2)),
      contentType: 'application/json',
    });
    expect(log.js, 'Uncaught browser JavaScript errors').toEqual([]);
  },
});
export { expect };

const norm = (s: string) =>
  s
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .replace(/đ/gi, 'd')
    .toLowerCase();

/** Browser-only interception. Never imported by src/ or shipped as a runtime adapter. */
export async function mockWorkspace(page: Page, user: 'A' | 'B' = 'A') {
  const uid = user === 'A' ? 6 : 7;
  const state = {
    tasks: structuredClone(tasks),
    messages: structuredClone(messages),
    bookmarks: [102],
    preferences: { view: 'list' },
    loggedIn: true,
    readIds: [402],
  };
  await page.clock.setFixedTime(new Date(fixedNow));
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    if (!localStorage.getItem('workspace-theme')) localStorage.setItem('workspace-theme', 'light');
  });
  await page.route('**/api/**', async (route) => {
    const req = route.request(),
      url = new URL(req.url()),
      path = url.pathname.replace('/api/', '');
    const method = req.method();
    const input = method === 'GET' ? {} : req.postDataJSON() || {};
    const ok = (data: unknown, status = 200) =>
      route.fulfill({ status, contentType: 'application/json', body: JSON.stringify(data) });
    if (path === 'health') return ok({ status: 'ok', database: 'ok' });
    if (path === 'auth/login') {
      state.loggedIn = true;
      return ok({ authenticated: true });
    }
    if (path === 'auth/logout') {
      state.loggedIn = false;
      return ok({ loggedOut: true });
    }
    if (!state.loggedIn)
      return ok({ error: { code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED', message: 'Vui lòng đăng nhập.' } }, 401);
    if (path === 'me')
      return ok({
        user: {
          ...users.find((u) => u.id === uid),
          login: `workspace.${user.toLowerCase()}@example.test`,
          partner_id: [uid + 100, 'Nhân viên'],
        },
        instance: 'visual-fixture',
        writesEnabled: true,
        hosting: 'self_hosted',
        odooUrl: 'https://odoo.example.test',
        authMode: 'password',
      });
    if (path === 'dashboard')
      return ok({
        counts: { mine: 3, overdue: 1, activities: 1, unread: 1, projects: 2 },
        recent: state.tasks.slice(0, 4),
        projects,
      });
    if (path === 'projects') return ok({ items: projects, nextCursor: null });
    if (/^projects\/\d+$/.test(path))
      return ok(projects.find((p) => p.id === Number(path.split('/')[1])));
    if (path === 'task-options')
      return ok({ projects, stages, users, states: fields.state.selection });
    if (path === 'tasks') {
      let items = state.tasks;
      const p = url.searchParams;
      if (p.get('open') === 'true') items = items.filter((t) => t.state !== '1_done');
      if (p.get('mine') === 'true') items = items.filter((t) => t.user_ids.includes(uid));
      if (p.get('project'))
        items = items.filter((t) => t.project_id[0] === Number(p.get('project')));
      if (p.get('stage')) items = items.filter((t) => t.stage_id[0] === Number(p.get('stage')));
      if (p.get('assignee'))
        items = items.filter((t) => t.user_ids.includes(Number(p.get('assignee'))));
      if (p.get('state')) items = items.filter((t) => t.state === p.get('state'));
      if (p.get('overdue') === 'true')
        items = items.filter(
          (t) => t.date_deadline && t.date_deadline < '2026-10-23' && t.state !== '1_done',
        );
      if (p.get('q'))
        items = items.filter((t) =>
          norm(`${t.name} ${t.project_id[1]} ${t.id}`).includes(norm(p.get('q')!)),
        );
      return ok({
        items,
        users,
        states: fields.state.selection,
        nextCursor: null,
        scanLimited: false,
        scanned: state.tasks.length,
      });
    }
    if (path.startsWith('tasks/')) {
      const [, id, child] = path.split('/');
      const task = state.tasks.find((t) => t.id === Number(id));
      if (!task)
        return ok(
          {
            error: {
              code: 'NOT_FOUND',
              message: 'Không tìm thấy nhiệm vụ hoặc không còn quyền xem.',
            },
          },
          404,
        );
      if (method === 'PATCH') {
        Object.assign(task, input.values);
        return ok({ saved: true });
      }
      if (child === 'messages') {
        if (method === 'POST') {
          state.messages.push({
            id: 303 + state.messages.length,
            body: `<p>${input.text}</p>`,
            author_id: [uid, users.find((u) => u.id === uid)!.name],
            date: '2026-10-23 02:00:00',
            message_type: 'comment',
            subtype_id: [1, 'Bình luận'],
          });
          return ok({ id: state.messages.at(-1)!.id });
        }
        return ok(state.messages);
      }
      if (child === 'activities')
        return ok([
          {
            id: 71,
            summary: 'Rà soát bản vẽ kỹ thuật',
            date_deadline: '2026-10-24',
            user_id: [6, users[0].name],
            activity_type_id: [1, 'Cần làm'],
          },
        ]);
      if (child === 'attachments')
        return ok([
          {
            id: 501,
            name: 'Ho-so-thiet-ke.pdf',
            file_size: 248000,
            create_date: '2026-10-22 08:00:00',
            mimetype: 'application/pdf',
          },
        ]);
      const ancestors = [];
      let parent = Array.isArray(task.parent_id) ? Number(task.parent_id[0]) : 0;
      while (parent) {
        const row = state.tasks.find((t) => t.id === parent);
        if (!row) break;
        ancestors.unshift(row);
        parent = Array.isArray(row.parent_id) ? Number(row.parent_id[0]) : 0;
      }
      return ok({
        task,
        ancestors,
        parentUnavailable: false,
        children: state.tasks.filter(
          (t) => Array.isArray(t.parent_id) && t.parent_id[0] === task.id,
        ),
        siblings: [],
        stages,
        users,
        fields,
        canAtomicEdit: true,
        writesEnabled: true,
        canWrite: true,
        odooLink: `https://odoo.example.test/web#id=${task.id}&model=project.task`,
      });
    }
    if (path === 'activities')
      return ok([
        {
          id: 71,
          summary: 'Rà soát bản vẽ kỹ thuật',
          date_deadline: '2026-10-24',
          task: state.tasks[1],
          activity_type_id: [1, 'Cần làm'],
        },
      ]);
    if (path === 'notifications')
      return ok({
        available: true,
        items: [
          {
            id: 401,
            is_read: state.readIds.includes(401),
            notification_type: 'inbox',
            kind: 'task',
            targetName: tasks[1].name,
            task: state.tasks[1],
            message: { ...state.messages[1], body: state.messages[1].body.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '') },
            href: '/tasks/102?tab=discussion&message=302',
          },
          {
            id: 402,
            is_read: state.readIds.includes(402),
            notification_type: 'inbox',
            kind: 'channel',
            targetName: 'Đội thiết kế',
            channel: channels[0],
            message: { ...state.messages[0], body: state.messages[0].body.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '') },
            href: '/discuss/41?message=301',
          },
        ],
      });
    if (path.startsWith('notifications/')) {
      state.readIds.push(Number(path.split('/')[1]));
      return ok({ saved: true });
    }
    if (path === 'ui-preferences') {
      if (method === 'POST') {
        Object.assign(state.preferences, input);
        return ok({ saved: true });
      }
      return ok(state.preferences);
    }
    if (path === 'preferences') {
      if (method === 'POST') return ok({ saved: true });
      return ok({ items: state.tasks.filter((t) => state.bookmarks.includes(t.id)) });
    }
    if (path === 'discuss') return ok({ available: true, items: channels });
    if (path.startsWith('discuss/'))
      return ok({ channel: channels[0], items: state.messages, nextCursor: null, focused: null });
    // Unknown APIs fail loudly instead of falling through to real Odoo.
    return ok(
      { error: { code: 'UNMOCKED_API', message: `Test fixture has no ${method} ${path}` } },
      501,
    );
  });
  return state;
}

test('offline edit keeps draft, reconnect permits explicit save, and errors never claim success', async ({
  page,
  context,
}) => {
  const state = await mockWorkspace(page);
  await page.goto('/tasks/102');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' }).fill('Bản nháp khi mất mạng');
  await context.setOffline(true);
  await expect(page.getByText(/Đang ngoại tuyến/)).toBeVisible();
  // Fixture routes can fulfill offline requests; force the mutation to fail as a real disconnected network would.
  await page.route('**/api/tasks/102', (r) =>
    r.request().method() === 'PATCH' ? r.abort('internetdisconnected') : r.fallback(),
  );
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu vào Odoo', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: /Không thể kết nối/ })).toBeVisible();
  expect(state.tasks.find((t) => t.id === 102)?.description).not.toContain('Bản nháp khi mất mạng');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đóng, giữ bản nháp' }).click();
  await context.setOffline(false);
  await page.reload();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Mô tả công việc' })).toContainText(
    'Bản nháp khi mất mạng',
  );
  await page.unroute('**/api/tasks/102');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu vào Odoo', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Bản nháp khi mất mạng', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  expect(state.tasks.find((t) => t.id === 102)?.description).toContain('Bản nháp khi mất mạng');
});

  await page.reload();

});
for (const view of views) {
  test(`WCAG A/AA automated checks: ${view.name}`, async ({ page }) => {
    await mockWorkspace(page);
    await page.goto(view.url);
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: view.ready, exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('.skeleton')).toHaveCount(0);
    await page.evaluate(() => document.fonts.ready);
    const result = await new AxeBuilder({ page })
      .withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa'])
      .analyze();
    expect(result.violations).toEqual([]);
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 844 });
    if (view.name !== 'login')
      await expect(page.locator('#workspace-sidebar')).toHaveAttribute('inert', '');
    for (const theme of ['light', 'dark']) {
      await page.evaluate((theme) => {
        document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
      }, theme);
      const mobile = await new AxeBuilder({ page })
        .withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa'])
        .analyze();
      expect(mobile.violations, `mobile ${theme}`).toEqual([]);
    }
  });
}

  const state = await mockWorkspace(page);

  await editor.fill('Nội dung định dạng');
  await editor.press('ControlOrMeta+a');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'In đậm', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'In đậm', exact: true })).toHaveAttribute(
    'aria-pressed',
    'true',
  );
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu vào Odoo', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('.task-main .rich-text strong')).toHaveText('Nội dung định dạng');
  expect(state.tasks.find((t) => t.id === 102)?.description).toContain('<strong>');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
  await editor.fill('Bỏ nội dung này');
  page.once('dialog', (dialog) => dialog.accept());
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Bỏ bản nháp' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Chỉnh sửa nhiệm vụ', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(editor).toContainText('Nội dung định dạng');
  await expect(editor).not.toContainText('Bỏ nội dung này');
});

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/unit/search.test.ts =====
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { highlightRanges, normalizeText } from '../../src/lib/search';
import { taskDomain } from '../../src/server/workspace';
import { Odoo, type Fields } from '../../src/server/odoo';
const metadata = {
  state: {
    string: 'State',
    type: 'selection',
    selection: [
      ['01_in_progress', 'Đang làm'],
      ['1_done', 'Xong'],
      ['1_canceled', 'Hủy'],
    ],
  },
  date_deadline: { string: 'Deadline', type: 'date' },
} as Fields;
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());
describe('Vietnamese search highlighting', () => {
  it('finds accentless partial matches and preserves original glyphs', () => {
    const text = 'Đối chiếu hồ sơ, kiểm tra HỒ SƠ';
    expect(highlightRanges(text, 'ho so').map(([a, b]) => text.slice(a, b))).toEqual([
      'hồ sơ',
      'HỒ SƠ',
    ]);
    expect(normalizeText('ĐƯỜNG dẫn')).toBe('duong dan');
  });
  it('does not split decomposed accents or emoji graphemes', () => {
    const text = '📐 Hồ sơ';
    const ranges = highlightRanges(text, 'ho so');
    expect(ranges).toEqual([[3, text.length]]);
    expect(text.slice(...ranges[0])).toBe('Hồ sơ');
    expect(highlightRanges(text, '')).toEqual([]);
    expect(highlightRanges(text, 'absent')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
describe('task filter boundaries', () => {
  it('uses the employee timezone for overdue and excludes terminal states', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2026-10-22T18:00:00Z'));
    const odoo = { read: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ tz: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' }) } as unknown as Odoo;
    const domain = await taskDomain(
      odoo,
      new URLSearchParams('mine=true&overdue=true&assignee=7'),
      6,
      metadata,
    );
    expect(domain).toContainEqual(['date_deadline', '<', '2026-10-23']);
    expect(domain).toContainEqual(['user_ids', 'in', [6]]);
    expect(domain).toContainEqual(['user_ids', 'in', [7]]);
    expect(domain).toContainEqual(['state', 'not in', ['1_done', '1_canceled']]);
  });
  it('rejects invalid relations and states before querying Odoo', async () => {
    const odoo = {} as Odoo;
    for (const query of ['project=-1', 'stage=bad', 'assignee=0', 'state=admin'])
      await expect(taskDomain(odoo, new URLSearchParams(query), 6, metadata)).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});

===== END FILE: tests/unit/search.test.ts =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/unit/security.test.ts =====
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { encrypt, decrypt } from '../../src/server/crypto';
import { normalize, cleanHtml, editTaskSchema } from '../../src/server/workspace';
import { config } from '../../src/server/config';
import { csrf } from '../../src/server/http';

const previous = { ...process.env };
afterEach(() => {
  process.env = { ...previous };
});
function env() {
  process.env.ODOO_URL = 'https://odoo.example.test';
  process.env.ODOO_DB = 'staging';
  process.env.ENCRYPTION_KEY = 'a'.repeat(64);
  process.env.APP_ORIGIN = 'https://workspace.example.test';
}
describe('credentials and request boundaries', () => {
  it('encrypts with fresh nonces and binds ciphertext to the user and instance', () => {
    const key = 'a'.repeat(64),
      secret = 'private-value-not-in-browser';
    const a = encrypt(secret, key, 'instance:1'),
      b = encrypt(secret, key, 'instance:1');
    expect(a).not.toBe(b);
    expect(a).not.toContain(secret);
    expect(decrypt(a, key, 'instance:1')).toBe(secret);
    expect(() => decrypt(a, key, 'instance:2')).toThrow();
    const bytes = Buffer.from(a, 'base64');
    bytes[31] ^= 1;
    expect(() => decrypt(bytes.toString('base64'), key, 'instance:1')).toThrow();
  });
  it('rejects HTTP, embedded credentials and arbitrary URL paths by default', () => {
    env();
    expect(config().write).toBe(false);
    for (const url of [
      'http://odoo.example.test',
      'https://user:pass@odoo.example.test',
      'https://odoo.example.test/web#action=1',
      'file:///etc/passwd',
    ]) {
      process.env.ODOO_URL = url;
      expect(() => config()).toThrow();
    }
  });
  it('requires both exact origin and non-simple header on mutations', () => {
    env();
    expect(() =>
      csrf(
        new Request('https://workspace.example.test/api/tasks', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { origin: 'https://evil.test', 'x-workspace-request': '1' },
        }),
      ),
    ).toThrow();
    expect(() =>
      csrf(
        new Request('https://workspace.example.test/api/tasks', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { origin: 'https://workspace.example.test' },
        }),
      ),
    ).toThrow();
    expect(() =>
      csrf(
        new Request('https://workspace.example.test/api/tasks', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { origin: 'https://workspace.example.test', 'x-workspace-request': '1' },
        }),
      ),
    ).not.toThrow();
  });
});
describe('Odoo content and search', () => {
  it('matches Vietnamese accents and đ consistently across case and whitespace', () => {
    expect(normalize('  KIỂM TRA HỒ SƠ  ')).toBe('kiem tra ho so');
    expect(normalize('Đối chiếu THIẾT KẾ')).toBe('doi chieu thiet ke');
    expect(normalize('<p>Kiểm tra hồ sơ</p>')).toBe('kiem tra ho so');
  });
  it('removes active HTML, event handlers, CSS and unsafe links from Odoo HTML', () => {
    const html = cleanHtml(
      '<script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=alert(1)><p style="display:none" onclick="alert(1)">Text</p><a href="javascript:alert(1)">click</a><iframe src="https://evil.test"></iframe>',
    );
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/script|onerror|onclick|style=|iframe|javascript:/);
    expect(html).toContain('<p>Text</p>');
  });
  it('rejects unknown write fields and invalid dates or relations', () => {
    expect(
      editTaskSchema.safeParse({ expectedRevision: 'r', values: { name: 'test', project_id: 1 } })
        .success,
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      editTaskSchema.safeParse({ expectedRevision: 'r', values: { date_deadline: '2026-99-99' } })
        .success,
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      editTaskSchema.safeParse({ expectedRevision: 'r', values: { user_ids: [-1] } }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});

===== END FILE: tests/unit/security.test.ts =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/visual/snapshots/README.md =====
# Renderer-specific screenshots

Chromium, fixture, font files, viewport, locale, timezone and animation settings are fixed by Playwright. Debian 13 and Ubuntu 24.04 still produce small differences in variable-font rasterization. `playwright.config.ts` selects snapshots using `/etc/os-release`; CI pins Ubuntu 24.04.

- `debian-13/`: original reviewed local production screenshots, preserved unchanged.
- `ubuntu-24.04/`: reviewed actual screenshots from CI run [39978725935](https://github.com/Phuongle29-01/odoo-workspace/actions/runs/39978725935), source application commit `da2a4d2`. The run's 4 real Odoo and 14 UI tests passed; 17 screenshots differed by 55–227 pixels, concentrated on glyph edges, with identical dimensions and layout. Four comparisons were already within the existing 50-pixel allowance. `provenance.json` records each source.

The comparison keeps `maxDiffPixels: 50`, without masks or a relaxed color threshold. Updates require explicit `UPDATE_VISUALS=1` after reviewing expected/actual/diff; CI never updates images. New OS versions need their own reviewed baseline rather than overwriting another renderer's reference.

===== END FILE: tests/visual/snapshots/README.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/visual/workspace.spec.ts =====
import { test, expect } from '../fixtures/diagnostics';
import { mockWorkspace, views } from '../fixtures/workspace';
for (const view of views) {
  test(`${view.name} visual`, async ({ page }, testInfo) => {
    await mockWorkspace(page);
    await page.goto(view.url);
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: view.ready, exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('.skeleton')).toHaveCount(0);
    await page.evaluate(() => document.fonts.ready);
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot(`${view.name}.png`, { fullPage: true });
    await testInfo.attach('review-screenshot', {
      body: await page.screenshot({ fullPage: true, animations: 'disabled' }),
      contentType: 'image/png',
    });
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth)).toBe(
      true,
    );
  });
}

===== END FILE: tests/visual/workspace.spec.ts =====
